THE VIDESA SEVA PADAKKAMA

IT is here by notified that His Excellency the President of the Democratic Public of Sri Lanka and Commander - in- chief of the Armed Forces has been graciously pleased to approve the awarding of the Videsa Seva Paddakkama interms of regulation four of the proclamation instating. constituting and creating the Videsa Seva Paddakkama Publishedin the Gazette Extraordinary, No.156/5 of 01st September 1981 to the person named in the schedule hereto.

By His Excellency’s Command,

KAPILA WAIDYARATHNE, P. C.,Secretary,Ministry of Defence.At Colombo,30th October, 2018.
